        function isConsentGiven (siteId,cookiePolicyId){
            var cs = document.cookie.split(';');
            for (var i = 0; i < cs.length; i++) {
                while (cs[i].charAt(0) == ' ') cs[i] = cs[i].substring(1);
                if(cs[i].indexOf('_iub_cs-s'+ siteId) == 0||cs[i].indexOf('_iub_cs-'+ cookiePolicyId) == 0) return true;
            }
            return false;
        }
    </script>

<script type="text/javascript">
    function scriptsLoader(){
        var me = this;
        me.scripts;
        me.scrollOffset;
        me.loadTimeout;
        me.callback;
        me.ctrl = false;

        me.init = function(conf){
            me.scripts = conf.scripts || null;
            me.scrollOffset = conf.scrollOffset || null;
            me.loadTimeout = conf.loadTimeout || null;
            me.callback = conf.callback || null;

            if(!me.scripts){
                return;
            }

            if(me.scrollOffset || me.loadTimeout){
                if(me.scrollOffset){
                    window.addEventListener("scroll", function(){
                        if(!me.ctrl && window.pageYOffset > me.scrollOffset){
                            me.ctrl = true;
                            me.loadScript();
                        }
                    }, false);
                }

                if(me.loadTimeout){
                    setTimeout(function() {
                        if(!me.ctrl){
                            me.ctrl = true;
                            me.loadScript();
                        }
                    }, me.loadTimeout);
                }
                return;
            }

            me.loadScript();
        };

        me.loader = function(scriptObj, handler){
            if(scriptObj.src){
                var script = document.createElement("script");
                script.src = scriptObj.src;
                script.async = scriptObj.async || null;
                script.async = scriptObj.defer || null;

                script.onload = script.onreadystatechange = function(){
                    script.onreadystatechange = script.onload = null;
                    handler();
                }

                document.head.appendChild(script);
            }
        };

        me.loadScript = function(){
            if(me.scripts.length != 0){
                me.loader(me.scripts.shift(), me.loadScript);
            } else {
                if(me.callback) {
                    window[me.callback]();
                }
            }
        }
    }
    </script>
<!-- END SCRIPTS LOADER -->

            { src: '/js/swiper.min.js?cb=1571837367', async: true },
            { src: '/js/swiperHelper.js?cb=1571837367', async: true }
        ],
        callback: "initSwiperHelper"
    };

    var swiperChainHandler = new scriptsLoader();
    swiperChainHandler.init(swiperChain);

    function initSwiperHelper(){
        swiperHelper = new swiperHelper();
        if(isCSSLoaded){
            swiperHelper.init(200);
            if(!lazySizesConfig.init){
                lazySizes.init();
            }
        } else {
            document.addEventListener("loadedCSS", function(){
                swiperHelper.init(200);
                if(!lazySizesConfig.init){
                    lazySizes.init();
                }
            });
        }
    }
</script>
<script class="_iub_cs_activate" type="text/plain">
        function metarefresh()  {
            var mr = this;
            mr.isok = true;
            mr.doit = function() {
                var a = setInterval(function(){ mr.doWhat(); }, 240000);
            }
            mr.doWhat = function() {
                if (mr.isok) {
                    window.location.reload();
                }
            }
        }
        var mere = new metarefresh();
        mere.doit();
        window.onfocus = function () {
            mere.isok = true;
        };
        window.onblur = function () {
            mere.isok = false;
        };
    </script>
